# Alexa wire-model inventory

Generated by `go run ./tools/modelinventory -write`. The gate checks every named schema component against a generated declaration and compares each object component's JSON field set to the generated Go shape, including inline nested objects. Source-derived API contracts remain implementation-derived unless separately verified.

## Handwritten semantic and input models

These exact type-level exceptions are handwritten because they are public SDK projections, caller inputs, local state, or library error types. Each declaration carries a matching `modelinventory` marker naming the type and reason. Provider wire fields remain generated from the schemas above. The gate rejects unlisted handwritten serialized structs, and it rejects anonymous nested JSON objects even when a marker is present.

| Type | Fields | Classification and reason |
|---|---|---|
| `pkg/alexaapimodels/auth.go.CodePairResponse` | privateCode, publicCode | `semantic`: CodePairResponse: browser login codes returned by the code-pair endpoint.; evidence: pkg/alexa/client.go#GenerateCodePair |
| `pkg/alexaapimodels/auth.go.RegistrationResponse` | accessToken, refreshToken | `semantic`: RegistrationResponse: access and refresh credentials returned after device pairing.; evidence: pkg/alexa/client.go#RegisterWithCodePair |
| `pkg/alexaapimodels/auth.go.TokenRefreshRequest` | config, refreshToken | `client-input`: TokenRefreshRequest: refresh credential and device registration options passed into the explicit token exchange.; evidence: pkg/alexa/client.go#Client.RefreshAccessToken, pkg/alexa/client.go#Session.RefreshAccessToken, pkg/alexa/client_options_session_test.go#TestSessionRefreshReturnsCredentialsWithoutImplicitlyReplacingThem |
| `pkg/alexaapimodels/auth.go.TokenRefreshResponse` | accessToken, expiresInSeconds | `semantic`: TokenRefreshResponse: caller-facing access token and expiry projection from the generated OAuth response.; evidence: pkg/alexa/client.go#RefreshAccessToken |
| `pkg/alexaapimodels/auth.go.UserInfo` | countryOfResidence, effectiveMarketPlaceId, email, eulaAcceptance, features, fullName, hasActiveDopplers, id, marketPlaceDomainName, marketPlaceId, marketPlaceLocale | `semantic`: UserInfo: account profile fields returned by the SDK profile lookup.; evidence: pkg/alexa/client.go#GetUserInfo |
| `pkg/alexaapimodels/auth.go.UserInfoRequest` | csrfToken, platform, version | `client-input`: UserInfoRequest: optional platform, client version, and CSRF values mapped to the profile request.; evidence: pkg/alexa/client.go#Session.GetUserInfo, pkg/alexa/client_userinfo_test.go#TestGetUserInfoMapsCallerOptionsToRESTRequest |
| `pkg/alexaapimodels/controls.go.ControlActionPayload` | action, params | `client-input`: ControlActionPayload: action name and optional parameters passed to a feature action.; evidence: pkg/alexa/client_control.go#controlAction |
| `pkg/alexaapimodels/controls.go.ControlAnnouncementPayload` | locale, message, method, title | `client-input`: ControlAnnouncementPayload: announcement text, display method, title, and locale consumed by the announcement operation.; evidence: pkg/alexa/client_control.go#controlAnnouncement |
| `pkg/alexaapimodels/controls.go.ControlAudioPlayerPayload` | providerId, searchPhrase, timerSeconds | `client-input`: ControlAudioPlayerPayload: provider, search phrase, and optional timer passed to music playback.; evidence: pkg/alexa/client_control.go#controlMusic |
| `pkg/alexaapimodels/controls.go.ControlAudioPlayerURIPayload` | offsetInMilliseconds, title, uri | `client-input`: ControlAudioPlayerURIPayload: public media URI and optional title or playback offset passed to URI playback.; evidence: pkg/alexa/client_control.go#controlAudioPlayerURI |
| `pkg/alexaapimodels/controls.go.ControlBrightnessAdjustPayload` | delta | `client-input`: ControlBrightnessAdjustPayload: brightness delta consumed by the adjust-brightness operation.; evidence: pkg/alexa/client_control.go#controlBrightness |
